Keep bench@1 unchanged across firmware preparation. [[docs/setup.md#L1-L5]]\n- Historical run identity comes from the run notes, not timestamps alone. Candidate timestamps span different dates; retain them verbatim and flag the inconsistency for review. Do not derive initialization duration from them. [[docs/runs.md#L1-L10]] [[logs/boot-0413.log#L1-L4]]\n- Optional future plugin requests only: enabled esp32-esptool, program `esptool`, command `write-flash`, risk `device-write` (overwrites firmware/data); enabled serial-monitor, program `serial`, command `capture`, risk `device-read` (opens console; reset may restart board). [[docs/setup.md#L1-L5]] [[docs/boot-test.md#L1-L5]]\n- **Acceptance:** Ready event present AND no ERROR line. Missing ready or any ERROR fails boot@1. [[docs/boot-test.md#L1-L5]]\n- **Stop conditions:** Stop on identity/setup mismatch, unverified firmware preparation or missing prerequisite. If control fails, preserve evidence and pause the paired comparison for review rather than attributing failure solely to the candidate; this comparison pause is a planning safeguard.
